- **b-cache-optimizer**: Use when optimizing Anthropic prompt caching in MindBridge, debugging low cache hit rates, or structuring the system prompt and patient context blocks for maximum cache reuse. Covers the 5-minute TTL constraint, ephemeral cache_control, and how to measure cache savings on Message.cachedInputTokens. ([Dr-Snatch/mindbridge-hackathon](https://github.com/Dr-Snatch/mindbridge-hackathon/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/b-cache-optimizer.md))
- **b-coping-injector**: Use when building the coping plan retrieval and injection system. Covers matching a patient's current message to their therapist-written coping plans, the embedding-based retrieval pipeline, the MVP LIKE-match fallback, and how plans get woven into the conversation agent's context block without feeling clinical. ([Dr-Snatch/mindbridge-hackathon](https://github.com/Dr-Snatch/mindbridge-hackathon/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/b-coping-injector.md))
- **b-crisis-detector**: Use when building, testing, or updating the MindBridge crisis detection system. Covers the deterministic lexicon, regex patterns, the Haiku async backup classifier, canned response text, and flag emission. This is the highest-priority component in the entire system — it ships before anything else. ([Dr-Snatch/mindbridge-hackathon](https://github.com/Dr-Snatch/mindbridge-hackathon/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/b-crisis-detector.md))

([andreaswiren/boil](https://github.com/andreaswiren/boil/blob/HEAD/boilerplates/base-windows-rust-app/.claude/agents/B08-service-autostart.md))
- **B09-updater**: Dispatch in Wave 3, after the contract freeze at H3, to build the auto-updater — signed manifest fetch, signature verification against an embedded public key, artefact hash and Authenticode checks, downgrade refusal, an atomic power-loss-safe swap, channels, stagger, restart handling, policy pinning and never-silent failure. It owns replacing installed files and nothing else. ([andreaswiren/boil](https://github.com/andreaswiren/boil/blob/HEAD/boilerplates/base-windows-rust-app/.claude/agents/B09-updater.md))

- **babeldub-translator**: BabelDub dub translator: translates or adapts one batch of lines (a translate/batches/*.in.jsonl file) so the text fits the voice and emotion of the original hero, stays within the syllable budget and keeps placeholders and the glossary, and corrects the measured delivery tags of a line where the scene calls for another delivery; submits the result with babeldub batch submit. - **babok-stage-5**: Agent Stage 5 BABOK — Future State Design (TO-BE). Użyj po zatwierdzeniu Stage 4. Projektuje architekturę rozwiązania i procesy przyszłego stanu. - **babok-stage-6**: Agent Stage 6 BABOK — Gap Analysis & Implementation Roadmap. Użyj po zatwierdzeniu Stage 5. - **babok-stage-7**: Agent Stage 7 BABOK — Risk Assessment & Mitigation. Użyj po zatwierdzeniu Stage 6 (lub równolegle z Stage 2 dla wstępnego skanu ryzyka).
